#Since random is not actually random, options are limited. 
#Random is only calculated once per event/gametime, so probability within the same event is NOT multiplicative

#Yugoslavia
event = {
	id = 4000
	random = no
	#country = GER
	persistent = yes
	one_action = yes

	trigger = {
		country = GER
		OR = {
			NOT = { alliance = { country = YUG } }
			ispuppet = YUG
			random = 25 #limit to 25
		}
		OR = {
			area = { area = "Croatia" 				country = -3 data = 1 value = 30 }
			area = { area = "East Serbia" 			country = -3 data = 1 value = 30 }
			area = { area = "Central Serbia" 		country = -3 data = 1 value = 30 }
			area = { area = "Bosnia" 				country = -3 data = 1 value = 30 }
		}
		OR = {
			random = 10 
			AND = {
				area = { area = "Croatia" 				country = -3 data = 1 value = 20 }
				area = { area = "East Serbia" 			country = -3 data = 1 value = 20 }
				area = { area = "Central Serbia" 		country = -3 data = 1 value = 20 }
				area = { area = "Bosnia" 				country = -3 data = 1 value = 20 }
				random = 15
			}
			AND = {
				area = { area = "Croatia" 				country = -3 data = 1 value = 30 }
				area = { area = "East Serbia" 			country = -3 data = 1 value = 30 }
				area = { area = "Central Serbia" 		country = -3 data = 1 value = 30 }
				area = { area = "Bosnia" 				country = -3 data = 1 value = 30 }
				random = 19
			}
			AND = {
				area = { area = "Croatia" 				country = -3 data = 1 value = 40 }
				area = { area = "East Serbia" 			country = -3 data = 1 value = 40 }
				area = { area = "Central Serbia" 		country = -3 data = 1 value = 40 }
				area = { area = "Bosnia" 				country = -3 data = 1 value = 40 }
				random = 23
			}
			AND = {
				area = { area = "Croatia" 				country = -3 data = 1 value = 50 }
				area = { area = "East Serbia" 			country = -3 data = 1 value = 50 }
				area = { area = "Central Serbia" 		country = -3 data = 1 value = 50 }
				area = { area = "Bosnia" 				country = -3 data = 1 value = 50 }
				random = 27
			}
			AND = {
				area = { area = "Croatia" 				country = -3 data = 1 value = 60 }
				area = { area = "East Serbia" 			country = -3 data = 1 value = 60 }
				area = { area = "Central Serbia" 		country = -3 data = 1 value = 60 }
				area = { area = "Bosnia" 				country = -3 data = 1 value = 60 }
				random = 30
			}
			AND = {
				area = { area = "Croatia" 				country = -3 data = 1 value = 70 }
				area = { area = "East Serbia" 			country = -3 data = 1 value = 70 }
				area = { area = "Central Serbia" 		country = -3 data = 1 value = 70 }
				area = { area = "Bosnia" 				country = -3 data = 1 value = 70 }
				random = 34
			}
			AND = {
				area = { area = "Croatia" 				country = -3 data = 1 value = 80 }
				area = { area = "East Serbia" 			country = -3 data = 1 value = 80 }
				area = { area = "Central Serbia" 		country = -3 data = 1 value = 80 }
				area = { area = "Bosnia" 				country = -3 data = 1 value = 80 }
				random = 37
			}
			AND = {
				area = { area = "Croatia" 				country = -3 data = 1 value = 90 }
				area = { area = "East Serbia" 			country = -3 data = 1 value = 90 }
				area = { area = "Central Serbia" 		country = -3 data = 1 value = 90 }
				area = { area = "Bosnia" 				country = -3 data = 1 value = 90 }
				random = 40
			}
			AND = {
				dissent = 5
				random = 15
			}
			AND = {
				dissent = 10
				random = 19
			}
			AND = {
				dissent = 15
				random = 23
			}
			AND = {
				dissent = 20
				random = 27
			}
			AND = {
				lost_national = { value = 5 }
				random = 15
			}
			AND = {
				lost_national = { value = 10 }
				random = 19
			}
			AND = {
				lost_national = { value = 15 }
				random = 23
			}
			AND = {
				lost_national = { value = 20 }
				random = 27
			}
		}
	}

	date = { day = 0 month = january year = 1936 }		 
	offset = 10
	deathdate = { day = 29 month = september year = 1999 }	

	name = EVT_4000_NAME
	desc = EVT_4000_DESC
	
	action = {
		name = EVT_4000_ACT1
		trigger = { OR = { control = { province = 383 data = -3 } control = { province = 383 data = REB } } }

event = {
	id = 4001
	random = no
	#country = GER
	persistent = yes
	one_action = yes

	trigger = {
		country = GER
		OR = {
			NOT = { alliance = { country = RUS } }
			ispuppet = RUS
		}
		region = { region = "Russia" 				country = -3 data = 1 value = 10 }
		#under usual circumstances, 40 or about once in 2 months
		OR = {
			random = 20 
			AND = {
				region = { region = "Russia"	 	country = -3 data = 1 value = 20 }
				random = 28
			}
			AND = {
				region = { region = "Russia"	 	country = -3 data = 1 value = 30 }
				random = 35
			}
			AND = {
				region = { region = "Russia"	 	country = -3 data = 1 value = 40 }
				random = 42
			}
			AND = {
				region = { region = "Russia"	 	country = -3 data = 1 value = 50 }
				random = 48
			}
			AND = {
				region = { region = "Russia"	 	country = -3 data = 1 value = 60 }
				random = 53
			}
			AND = {
				region = { region = "Russia"	 	country = -3 data = 1 value = 70 }
				random = 57
			}
			AND = {
				region = { region = "Russia"	 	country = -3 data = 1 value = 80 }
				random = 62
			}
			AND = {
				region = { region = "Russia"	 	country = -3 data = 1 value = 90 }
				random = 66
			}
			AND = {
				dissent = 5
				random = 28
			}
			AND = {
				dissent = 10
				random = 35
			}
			AND = {
				dissent = 15
				random = 42
			}
			AND = {
				dissent = 20
				random = 48
			}

				lost_national = { value = 5 }

			}
		}
	}

event = {
	id = 4002
	random = no
	#country = GER
	persistent = yes
	one_action = yes

	trigger = {
		country = GER
		OR = {
			NOT = { alliance = { country = RUS } }
			ispuppet = RUS
		}
		region = { region = "Russia" 				country = -3 data = 1 value = 10 }
		#under usual circumstances, 40 or about once in 2 months
		OR = {
			random = 20 
			AND = {
				region = { region = "Russia"	 	country = -3 data = 1 value = 20 }
				random = 28
			}
			AND = {
				region = { region = "Russia"	 	country = -3 data = 1 value = 30 }
				random = 35
			}
			AND = {
				region = { region = "Russia"	 	country = -3 data = 1 value = 40 }
				random = 42
			}
			AND = {
				region = { region = "Russia"	 	country = -3 data = 1 value = 50 }
				random = 48
			}
			AND = {
				region = { region = "Russia"	 	country = -3 data = 1 value = 60 }
				random = 53
			}
			AND = {
				region = { region = "Russia"	 	country = -3 data = 1 value = 70 }
				random = 57
			}
			AND = {
				region = { region = "Russia"	 	country = -3 data = 1 value = 80 }
				random = 62
			}
			AND = {
				region = { region = "Russia"	 	country = -3 data = 1 value = 90 }
				random = 66
			}
			AND = {
				dissent = 5
				random = 28
			}
			AND = {
				dissent = 10
				random = 35
			}
			AND = {
				dissent = 15
				random = 42
			}
			AND = {
				dissent = 20
				random = 48
			}
			AND = {
				lost_national = { value = 5 }
				random = 28
			}
			AND = {
				lost_national = { value = 10 }
				random = 35
			}

				lost_national = { value = 15 }
				random = 42
			}

				lost_national = { value = 20 }
				random = 48
			}
		}
	}

		}

			region 	= { region = "Belarus" 					country = -3 data = 1 value = 20 }

	id = 4004
	random = no
	#country = GER
	persistent = yes
	one_action = yes

	trigger = {
		country = GER
		OR = {
			NOT = { alliance = { country = UKR } }
			ispuppet = UKR
		}
		region 	= { region = "Ukraine" 						country = -3 data = 1 value = 10 }
		#under usual circumstances, 50
		OR = {
			random = 4 
			AND = {
				region 	= { region = "Ukraine" 				country = -3 data = 1 value = 20 }
				random = 6
			}
			AND = {
				region 	= { region = "Ukraine" 				country = -3 data = 1 value = 30 }
				random = 8
			}
			AND = {
				region 	= { region = "Ukraine" 				country = -3 data = 1 value = 40 }
				random = 10
			}
			AND = {
				region 	= { region = "Ukraine" 				country = -3 data = 1 value = 50 }
				random = 11
			}
			AND = {
				region 	= { region = "Ukraine" 				country = -3 data = 1 value = 60 }
				random = 13
			}
			AND = {
				region 	= { region = "Ukraine" 				country = -3 data = 1 value = 70 }
				random = 15
			}

			}

				random = 18
			}

				dissent = 5
				random = 6
			}

				dissent = 10
				random = 8
			}

			}

	}

	one_action = yes

		country = GER
		OR = {
			NOT = { alliance = { country = GRE } }
			ispuppet = GRE
		}
		OR = {
			area 	= { area = "Albania" 					country = -3 data = 1 value = 80 }
			area 	= { area = "Thrace" 					country = -3 data = 1 value = 10 }

	one_action = yes

		country = GER
		OR = {
			NOT = { alliance = { country = POL } }
			ispuppet = POL
		}
		region = { region = "Poland" 						country = -3 data = 1 value = 30 }
		OR = {
			random = 2
			AND = {
				region = { region = "Poland" 				country = -3 data = 1 value = 40 }
				random = 4
			}
			AND = {
				region = { region = "Poland" 				country = -3 data = 1 value = 50 }
				random = 6
			}
			AND = {
				region = { region = "Poland" 				country = -3 data = 1 value = 60 }
				random = 8
			}
			AND = {
				region = { region = "Poland" 				country = -3 data = 1 value = 70 }
				random = 10
			}
			#Part of Poland region is ethnic Germans/Belarussians/Ukrainians
			AND = {
				dissent = 5
				random = 6
			}
			AND = {
				dissent = 10
				random = 8
			}
			AND = {
				dissent = 15
				random = 10
			}

				dissent = 20
				random = 11
			}

event = {
	id = 4009
	random = no
	#TAG = { ENG GER }
	persistent = yes
	one_action = yes

	trigger = {
		OR = {
			country = ENG
			country = GER
		}
		OR = {
			NOT = { 
				alliance = { country = SPA }
				alliance = { country = SPR }
			}
			ispuppet = SPA
			ispuppet = SPR
			random = 15 #limit to 15
		}
		region 	= { region = "Spain" 						country = -3 data = 1 value = 5  }
		OR = {
			random = 5 
			AND = {
				region 	= { region = "Spain" 				country = -3 data = 1 value = 10 }
				random = 10
			}
			AND = {
				region 	= { region = "Spain" 				country = -3 data = 1 value = 20 }
				random = 15
			}
			AND = {
				region 	= { region = "Spain" 				country = -3 data = 1 value = 30 }
				random = 19
			}
			AND = {
				region 	= { region = "Spain" 				country = -3 data = 1 value = 40 }
				random = 23
			}
			AND = {
				region 	= { region = "Spain" 				country = -3 data = 1 value = 50 }
				random = 27
			}
			AND = {
				region 	= { region = "Spain" 				country = -3 data = 1 value = 60 }
				random = 30
			}
			AND = {
				region 	= { region = "Spain" 				country = -3 data = 1 value = 70 }
				random = 34
			}
			AND = {
				region 	= { region = "Spain" 				country = -3 data = 1 value = 80 }
				random = 37
			}
			AND = {
				region 	= { region = "Spain" 				country = -3 data = 1 value = 90 }
				random = 40
			}
			AND = {
				dissent = 5
				random = 15
			}
			AND = {
				dissent = 10
				random = 23
			}
			AND = {
				dissent = 15
				random = 27
			}
			AND = {
				dissent = 20
				random = 30
			}
			AND = {
				lost_national = { value = 5 }
				random = 15
			}
			AND = {
				lost_national = { value = 10 }
				random = 23
			}
			AND = {
				lost_national = { value = 15 }
				random = 27
			}
			AND = {
				lost_national = { value = 20 }
				random = 30
			}
		}
	}

event = {
	id = 4010
	random = no
	#country = GER
	persistent = yes
	one_action = yes

	trigger = {
		country = GER
		OR = {
			NOT = { alliance = { country = POR } }
			ispuppet = POR
			random = 15 #limit to 15
		}
		
		region 	= { region = "Portugal" 						country = -3 data = 1 value = 5  }
		OR = {
			random = 5 
			AND = {
				region 	= { region = "Portugal" 				country = -3 data = 1 value = 10 }
				random = 10
			}
			AND = {
				region 	= { region = "Portugal" 				country = -3 data = 1 value = 20 }
				random = 15
			}
			AND = {
				region 	= { region = "Portugal" 				country = -3 data = 1 value = 30 }
				random = 19
			}
			AND = {
				region 	= { region = "Portugal" 				country = -3 data = 1 value = 40 }
				random = 23
			}
			AND = {
				region 	= { region = "Portugal" 				country = -3 data = 1 value = 50 }
				random = 27
			}
			AND = {
				region 	= { region = "Portugal" 				country = -3 data = 1 value = 60 }
				random = 30
			}
			AND = {
				region 	= { region = "Portugal" 				country = -3 data = 1 value = 70 }
				random = 34
			}
			AND = {
				region 	= { region = "Portugal" 				country = -3 data = 1 value = 80 }
				random = 37
			}

				region 	= { region = "Portugal" 				country = -3 data = 1 value = 90 }
				random = 40
			}

				dissent = 5
				random = 15

				dissent = 20

			}

				lost_national = { value = 15 }
				random = 27
			}
			AND = {
				lost_national = { value = 20 }
				random = 30
			}
		}
	}

	offset = 10
	deathdate = { day = 29 month = september year = 1999 }

	desc = EVT_4010_DESC

		name = EVT_4010_ACT1
		trigger = { OR = { control = { province = 335 data = -3 } control = { province = 335 data = REB } } }

#Caucasus SOV version
event = {
	id = 4050
	random = no
	#country = SOV
	persistent = yes
	one_action = yes

	trigger = {
		country = SOV
		NOT = { event = 3183521 } #Chechens deported
		OR = {
			year = 1941
			war = { country = FIN } #Chechen uprising was inspired by the Finnish resistance
		}
		OR = {
			control = { province = 7313 data = -3 }
			control = { province = 1847 data = -3 }
			control = { province = 2368 data = -3 }
			control = { province = 1844 data = -3 }
			control = { province = 3438 data = -3 }
			control = { province = 3437 data = -3 }
			control = { province = 7309 data = -3 }
		}
		OR = {
			random = 3
			AND = {
				dissent = 5
				random = 10
			}
			AND = {
				dissent = 10
				random = 15
			}
			AND = {
				dissent = 15
				random = 23
			}
			AND = {
				dissent = 20
				random = 27
			}
			AND = {
				lost_national = { value = 10 }
				random = 10
			}
			AND = {
				lost_national = { value = 15 }
				random = 15
			}
			AND = {
				lost_national = { value = 20 }
				random = 23
			}
			AND = {
				lost_national = { value = 25 }
				random = 27
			}
		}
	}

	date = { day = 0 month = january year = 1936 }		 
	offset = 10
	deathdate = { day = 29 month = september year = 1999 }	

	name = EVT_4050_NAME
	desc = EVT_4050_DESC

#Auto-Revolters in Europe


#Yugoslavia
event = {
	id = 4500
	random = no
	country = REB
	persistent = yes

	trigger = {
		NOT = { local_flag = YugRevolt }
		#war = { country = GER country = SOV }
		OR = {
			NOT = { exists = YUG }
			ispuppet = YUG
			lost_VP = { country = YUG value = 80 }
		}
		OR = {
			NOT = { exists = U27 }
			lost_national = { country = U27 value = 90 }
			NOT = { region = { region = "Balkan" country = U27 data = 1 value = 4 } } #~5 provinces
		}
		OR = {
			area = { area = "Croatia" 				data = 1 value = 50 }
			area = { area = "Bosnia" 				data = 1 value = 50 }
			area = { area = "Central Serbia"		data = 1 value = 50 }
			area = { area = "East Serbia"			data = 1 value = 50 }
			war = { country = GER country = SER }
			war = { country = GER country = CRO }
			AND = {
				OR = {
					alliance = { country = ROM country = SOV }
					alliance = { country = U30 country = SOV }
					region = { region = "Romania" 	country = SOV	data = 1 value = 80 }
				}
				OR = {
					alliance = { country = BUL country = SOV }
					alliance = { country = U29 country = SOV }
					area = { area = "Bulgaria" 		country = SOV	data = 1 value = 80 }
				}
			}
			AND = {
				NOT = { local_flag = YUG_Germany }
				#Historically September 1941, first major uprising
				OR = {
					event = { id = 2007041 days = 90  } #3 months from invasion of USSR
					event = { id = 203120  days = 360 } #A year from partitioning of Yugoslavia
				}
				OR = {
					garrison = { country = REB province = 453	type = land size = 9 area = 2 }
					AND = {
						random = 20
						OR = {
							garrison = { country = REB province = 453	type = land size = 3 area = 1 }
							garrison = { country = REB province = 5464	type = land size = 3 area = 1 }
							garrison = { country = REB province = 385	type = land size = 3 area = 1 }
						}
					}
				}
			}
			#AND = {
			#	exists = U27
			#	garrison = { country = REB province = 453	type = land size = 12 area = 2 } #this might include greece
			#}
		}
		#random = 20
	}

	date = { day = 0 month = january year = 1936 }		 
	offset = 10
	deathdate = { day = 29 month = september year = 1999 }	

	name = "AI_EVENT"
	
	action = {
		command = { type = local_setflag which = YugRevolt }
		
		command = { trigger = {
						OR = {
							event = { id = 2007041 days = 90  } #3 months from invasion of USSR
							event = { id = 203120  days = 360 } #A year from partitioning of Yugoslavia
						}
					}
					type = local_setflag which = YUG_Germany }
		
		#Partisan strength in 1941 was about 100,000.
		#Partisan peak strength in 1944 is ~650,000. 
		#Most rebel divisions start very understrength, average of about 30%. 36*10,000*0.30=108,000
		
		#total 36
		command = { trigger = { NOT = { control = { province = 5464	data = U27 } } } type = revolt which = 5464	when = 1 }
		command = { trigger = { NOT = { control = { province = 5464	data = U27 } } } type = revolt which = 5464	when = 1 }
		command = { trigger = { NOT = { control = { province = 5464	data = U27 } } } type = revolt which = 5464	when = 1 }
		
		command = { trigger = { NOT = { control = { province = 383	data = U27 } } } type = revolt which = 383	when = 1 }
		command = { trigger = { NOT = { control = { province = 383	data = U27 } } } type = revolt which = 383	when = 1 }
		command = { trigger = { NOT = { control = { province = 383	data = U27 } } } type = revolt which = 383	when = 1 }
		command = { trigger = { NOT = { control = { province = 385	data = U27 } } } type = revolt which = 385	when = 1 }
		command = { trigger = { NOT = { control = { province = 385	data = U27 } } } type = revolt which = 385	when = 1 }

	#country = U27
	persistent = yes

	desc = EVT_4502_DESC

		name = "ACTION_NAME_HURRAH"

		command = { trigger = { war = { country = GER country = CRO } } type = inherit which = CRO value = 1 } 
		command = { trigger = { war = { country = GER country = BOS } } type = inherit which = BOS value = 1 } 
		command = { trigger = { war = { country = GER country = MTN } } type = inherit which = MTN value = 1 }

#Albania
event = {
	id = 4510
	random = no
	country = REB
	persistent = yes

	trigger = {
		NOT = { local_flag = ALBRevolt }
		war = { country = GER country = SOV }
		OR = {
			NOT = { exists = ALB }
			ispuppet = ALB
		}
		OR = {
			NOT = { exists = U28 }
			NOT = { area = { area = "Albania" country = U28 data = 1 value = 30 } } # < 3 provinces
		}
		OR = {
			area = { area = "Albania" 				data = 1 value = 50 }
			AND = {
				OR = {
					alliance = { country = ROM country = SOV }
					alliance = { country = U30 country = SOV }
					region = { region = "Romania" 	country = SOV	data = 1 value = 80 }
				}
				OR = {
					alliance = { country = BUL country = SOV }
					alliance = { country = U29 country = SOV }
					area = { area = "Bulgaria" 		country = SOV	data = 1 value = 80 }
				}
				OR = {
					area = { area = "East Serbia" 	country = SOV	data = 1 value = 40 }
					area = { area = "East Serbia" 	country = U27	data = 1 value = 40 }
					random = 2
				}
			}
		}
		#random = 20
	}

	date = { day = 0 month = january year = 1936 }		 
	offset = 10
	deathdate = { day = 29 month = september year = 1999 }	

	name = "AI_EVENT"
	
	action = {
		command = { type = local_setflag which = ALBRevolt }
		#Partisan peak strength in 1944 is ~50,000. Most rebel divisions start very understrength
		command = { trigger = { NOT = { control = { province = 664	data = U28 } } } type = revolt which = 664	when = 1 }
		command = { trigger = { NOT = { control = { province = 664	data = U28 } } } type = revolt which = 664	when = 1 }

		command = { trigger = { NOT = { control = { province = 390	data = U28 } } } type = revolt which = 390	when = 1 }
		command = { trigger = { NOT = { control = { province = 391	data = U28 } } } type = revolt which = 391	when = 1 }
		command = { trigger = { NOT = { control = { province = 5465	data = U28 } } } type = revolt which = 5465	when = 1 }
		command = { trigger = { NOT = { control = { province = 7163	data = U28 } } } type = revolt which = 7163	when = 1 }
		
		command = { type = event which = 4511 where = REB when = 4 }
	}
}

#Greece
event = {
	id = 4520
	random = no
	country = REB
	persistent = yes

	trigger = {
		NOT = { local_flag = GRERevolt }
		war = { country = GER country = SOV }
		OR = {
			NOT = { exists = GRE }
			ispuppet = GRE
		}
		OR = {
			NOT = { exists = U33 }
			lost_VP = { country = U33 value = 80 }
			NOT = { region = { region = "Balkan" country = U33 data = 1 value = 2 } } # < 3 provinces
		}
		OR = {
			area = { area = "Thrace" 				data = 1 value = 50 }
			area = { area = "Attica" 				data = 1 value = 50 }
			war = { country = GER country = U66 }
			AND = {
				OR = {
					alliance = { country = ROM country = SOV }
					alliance = { country = U30 country = SOV }
					region = { region = "Romania" 	country = SOV	data = 1 value = 80 }
				}
				OR = {
					alliance = { country = BUL country = SOV }
					alliance = { country = U29 country = SOV }
					area = { area = "Bulgaria" 		country = SOV	data = 1 value = 80 }
				}
				OR = {
					area = { area = "East Serbia" 	country = SOV	data = 1 value = 50 }
					area = { area = "East Serbia" 	country = U27	data = 1 value = 50 }
					random = 2
				}
			}
			AND = {
				NOT = { local_flag = GRE_1943 }
				#Historically Early 1943, first major uprising
				event = { id = 2022025 days = 540 } #1.5 years from partitioning of Greece
				garrison = { country = REB province = 392	type = land size = 2 area = 1 }
			}
			AND = {
				exists = U33
				garrison = { country = REB province = 396	type = land size = 9 area = 1 }
			}
		}
		#random = 20
	}

	date = { day = 0 month = january year = 1936 }		 
	offset = 12
	deathdate = { day = 29 month = september year = 1999 }	

	name = "AI_EVENT"
	
	action = {
		command = { type = local_setflag which = GRERevolt }
		
		command = { trigger = {
						OR = {
							event = { id = 2007041 days = 90  } #3 months from invasion of USSR
							event = { id = 203120  days = 360 } #A year from partitioning of Yugoslavia
						}
				}	type = local_setflag which = GRE_1943 }
				
		command = { trigger = { war = { country = GER country = U66 } } type = inherit which = U66 value = 1 } #Pindus
		
		#Partisan peak strength in 1944 is ~80,000. Most rebel divisions start very understrength
		command = { trigger = { NOT = { control = { province = 392	data = U33 } } } type = revolt which = 392	when = 1 }
		command = { trigger = { NOT = { control = { province = 392	data = U33 } } } type = revolt which = 392	when = 1 }
		command = { trigger = { NOT = { control = { province = 392	data = U33 } } } type = revolt which = 392	when = 1 }

		command = { trigger = { NOT = { control = { province = 2319	data = U33 } } } type = revolt which = 2319	when = 1 }
		command = { trigger = { NOT = { control = { province = 2319	data = U33 } } } type = revolt which = 2319	when = 1 }
		
		command = { trigger = { NOT = { control = { province = 3392	data = U33 } } } type = revolt which = 3392	when = 1 }
		command = { trigger = { NOT = { control = { province = 3392	data = U33 } } } type = revolt which = 3392	when = 1 }
		
		command = { type = event which = 4521 where = REB when = 6 }
	}
}

event = {
	id = 4560
	random = no
	country = REB
	persistent = yes

	trigger = {
		NOT = { local_flag = U15Revolt }
		OR = {
			NOT = { exists = POR }
			ispuppet = POR
		}
		OR = {
			NOT = { exists = U15 }
			lost_VP = { country = U15 value = 80 }
			NOT = { region = { region = "Portugal" country = U15 data = 1 value = 15 } } # < 4 provinces
		}
		OR = {
			region = { region = "Portugal" 	country = REB	data = 1 	value = 30 }
			garrison = { country = REB province = 336		country = REB	type = land size = 15 area = 2 } #15 rebel divisions in region
			war = { country = GER country = SPR } #Spanish revolt
		}
	}

	date = { day = 0 month = january year = 1936 }

	name = "AI_EVENT"
	
	action = {

	id = 4570

	country = REB
	persistent = yes

		OR = {
			NOT = { exists = U42 }

			NOT = { area = { area = "Astrakhan" country = U42 data = 1 value = 1 } }
		}
		OR = {
			event = 3183520 #Soviets began deportations
			area = { area = "Astrakhan" 	country = REB	data = 1 	value = 15 } #4 provinces

event = {
	id = 4580
	random = no
	country = REB
	persistent = yes

	trigger = {
		NOT = { local_flag = UKRRevolt }
		OR = {
			NOT = { exists = UKR }
			ispuppet = UKR
			AND = {
				NOT = { region = { region = "Ukraine" 		country = UKR data = 1 value = 5  } }
				NOT = { area = { area = "Brest Litovsk" 	country = UKR data = 1 value = 10 } }
				NOT = { area = { area = "Lvov"			 	country = UKR data = 1 value = 10 } }
			}
		}

		OR = {
			region = { region = "Ukraine" 	country = REB data = 1 value = 10 } #6 provinces

							area = { area = "Lvov" 			country = REB data = 1 value = 70 } #7 provinces
							garrison = { country = REB province = 238		country = REB type = land size = 20 area = 1 } #20 rebel divisions in Ukraine region
						}
					}
					type = local_setflag which = GerWithdrawalUkraine }

		command = { trigger = { NOT = { control = { province = 3406  	data = UKR } } } type = revolt which = 3406	when = 1 }

#Baltic
event = {
	id = 4590
	random = no
	country = REB
	persistent = yes

	trigger = {
		NOT = { local_flag = BALRevolt }
		OR = {
			AND = {
				NOT = { exists = LIT }
				NOT = { exists = LAT }
				NOT = { exists = EST }
			}
			ispuppet = LIT
			ispuppet = LAT
			ispuppet = EST
			AND = {
				NOT = { area = { area = "Estonia" 		country = EST data = 1 value = 3 } }
				NOT = { area = { area = "Latvia" 		country = LAT data = 1 value = 3 } }
				NOT = { area = { area = "Lithuania" 	country = LIT data = 1 value = 3 } }
			}
		}
		OR = {
			region = { region = "Baltic" 	country = REB data = 1 value = 35 } #10 provinces
			garrison = { country = REB province = 197		country = REB type = land size = 25 area = 2 } #25 rebel divisions in Baltic region
			AND = {
				NOT = { local_flag = GerWithdrawalBaltic }
				NOT = { war = { country = SOV country = LIT } }
				NOT = { war = { country = SOV country = LAT } }
				NOT = { war = { country = SOV country = EST } }
				flag = { which = revolt_count_BLR when = 1 value = 15  } #Germany occupied Baltic at least for some time (Belarus closest)
				region = { region = "Baltic States" 	country = SOV data = 1 value = 90 }
				area = { area = "Lithuania" 			country = SOV data = 1 value = 90 }
				random = 30
			}
		}
	}

	date = { day = 0 month = january year = 1936 }		 
	offset = 10
	deathdate = { day = 29 month = september year = 1999 }	

	name = "AI_EVENT"
	
	action = {
		command = { type = local_setflag which = BALRevolt }
		
		command = { trigger = {
						NOT = { local_flag = GerWithdrawalBaltic }
						NOT = {
							region = { region = "Baltic" 	country = REB data = 1 value = 35 } #10 provinces
							garrison = { country = REB province = 197		country = REB type = land size = 25 area = 2 } #25 rebel divisions in Baltic region
						}

event = {
	id = 8200
	random = no
	#country = SOV
	persistent = yes

	decision = {
		country = SOV
		OR = {
			local_flag = partisan_mode
			war = { country = GER }
		}
		atwar = yes
		NOT = { dissent = 15 }
		NOT = { exists = U67 }
		garrison = { country = REB province = 238	type = land size = 3 area = 2 }
		#Making sure that these are anti-German partisans, not anti-Soviet
		region = { region = "Ukraine" 	country = GER data = 1 value = 20 }
	}
	decision_trigger = {
	}
	trigger = {
		country = SOV
		OR = {
			local_flag = partisan_mode
			war = { country = GER }
		}
		atwar = yes
		NOT = { dissent = 15 }
		NOT = { exists = U67 }
		region = { region = "Ukraine" 	country = GER data = 1 value = 20 }
		garrison = { country = REB province = 238	type = land size = 3 area = 2 }
	}

	date = { day = 0 month = january year = 1936 }		 
	offset = 10
	deathdate = { day = 29 month = september year = 1999 }	

	name = EVT_8200_NAME
	desc = EVT_8200_DESC
	picture = "sov_partisans"
	style = 2
	
	action = {
		name = "ACTION_NAME_OK" 
		trigger = { exists = UTO }
		command = { type = event which = 8201 where = REB when = 0 }
	}
	action = {
		name = "ACTION_NAME_OK" 
		trigger = { NOT = { exists = UTO } }
		command = { type = secedeprovince	which = SOV value = 7996		when = 2 }
		command = { type = independence		which = UTO value = 1 }

		}
	}

event = {
	id = 8204
	random = no
	#country = SOV
	persistent = yes

	decision = {
		country = SOV
		OR = {
			local_flag = partisan_mode
			war = { country = GER }
		}
		atwar = yes
		NOT = { dissent = 15 }
		NOT = { exists = U67 }
		garrison = { country = REB province = 175	type = land size = 3 area = 2 }
		#Partisans on the territory of Russia are always anti-foreign
	}
	decision_trigger = {
	}
	trigger = {
		country = SOV
		OR = {
			local_flag = partisan_mode
			war = { country = GER }
		}
		atwar = yes
		NOT = { dissent = 15 }
		NOT = { exists = U67 }
		garrison = { country = REB province = 175	type = land size = 3 area = 2 }
	}

	date = { day = 0 month = january year = 1936 }		 
	offset = 10
	deathdate = { day = 29 month = september year = 1999 }	

	name = EVT_8204_NAME
	desc = EVT_8204_DESC
	picture = "sov_partisans"
	style = 2
	
	action = {
		name = "ACTION_NAME_OK" 
		trigger = { exists = UTO }
		command = { type = event which = 8205 where = REB when = 0 }
	}
	action = {
		name = "ACTION_NAME_OK"

##

#Manchuria

event = {
	id = 8500
	random = no
	country = MAN
	persistent = yes

	name = EVT_8500_NAME
	desc = EVT_8500_DESC
	style = 2

	trigger = {
		NOT = { ai = JAP }
		NOT = { flag = ManRevoltCooldown }
		OR = {
			area = { area = "Jilin" 		country = -3 data = 1 value = 20 }
			area = { area = "Heilongjiang" 	country = -3 data = 1 value = 20 }
			area = { area = "Liaoning" 		country = -3 data = 1 value = 20 }
			area = { area = "Rehe" 			country = -3 data = 1 value = 20 }
		}
		OR = {
			NOT = { garrison = { country = MAN province = 2241 type = land size = 15 area = yes } }
			NOT = { garrison = { country = JAP province = 2241 type = land size = 15 area = yes } }
		}
	}

	date = { day = 1 month = march year = 1936 }
	offset = 15
	deathdate = { day = 20 month = november year = 1945 }

	action = {
		name = "ACTION_NAME_DAMN"
		command = { type = dissent 		 value = 0.5  }
		command = { type = manpowerpool  value = -5   }
		command = { type = supplies 	 value = -500 }
		
		command = { type = revolt which = -3 when = 1 }
		command = { type = revolt which = -3 when = 1 }
		command = { type = revolt which = -3 when = 1 }
		command = { type = revolt which = -3 when = 1 }
		command = { type = revolt which = -3 when = 1 }
		command = { type = revolt which = -3 when = 1 }
		command = { type = revolt which = -3 when = 1 }
		command = { type = revolt which = -3 when = 1 }
		command = { type = revolt which = -3 when = 1 }
		command = { type = revolt which = -3 when = 1 }
		
		command = { type = setflag which = ManRevoltCooldown }
		command = { type = event which = 8501 where = REB when = 20 } #at least 20 days in between revolts
		command = { type = event which = 8502 where = JAP when = 0  }
	}
}

		NOT = { war = { country = UKR } }
		NOT = { flag = UPARevoltCooldown }
		flag = { which = revolt_count_UKR when = 1 value = 10  } #Germany occupied Ukraine at least for some time
		area = { area = "Brest Litovsk" 	country = -3 data = 1 value = 90 }
		area = { area = "Lvov"			 	country = -3 data = 1 value = 90 }
		#it's okay if some territory is still held by Germany, since UPA attacked Germans too
		#On the other hand, if the frontline is too close, the soviet army will easily suppress the revolt

	}
